# 6.1.4 容量與排程資源不足

所屬：[6.1 根因的類型](6-1.md)

## 內文

#### 是什麼、什麼時候會用到

容量不足是指小區的無線資源（實體資源區塊 PRB、下行控制通道的 CCE）或連線數量撐不住當下的需求，排程器只好讓每個使用者分到較少的資源，或讓使用者排隊等待。它的特徵是**無線條件正常，但人一多就慢**：訊號強度與品質沒有變差，忙時的使用者速率卻明顯下降、延遲上升。維運與客服常在這些情況遇到：演唱會、跨年、運動賽事或展覽的熱點；通勤尖峰的車站；新社區大量入住後；客訴內容是「訊號滿格但網路很慢」，而且只在特定時段發生。判斷所需的量測主要定義在 TS 28.552（[TS 28.552](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/28552-ic0.zip)）。

#### 怎麼做／怎麼判斷

**1. 認得要看的量測（TS 28.552 V18.12.0）。** 量測名稱一字照原文：

| 量測（原文名稱） | 量測名 | 條號 | 重點 |
|---|---|---|---|
| DL Total PRB Usage／UL Total PRB Usage | `RRU.PrbDl`／`RRU.PrbUl` | 5.1.1.2.1、5.1.1.2.2 | 任何用途的 PRB 使用百分比，0～100 的整數，物件 NRCellDU |
| Distribution of DL Total PRB Usage／Distribution of UL total PRB usage | `RRU.PrbTotDlDist.Bin`／`RRU.PrbTotUlDist.Bin` | 5.1.1.2.3、5.1.1.2.4 | 以短時間（例如 1 ms）樣本統計的分布，抓得到瞬間滿載 |
| Mean DL PRB used for data traffic／DL total available PRB | `RRU.PrbUsedDl`／`RRU.PrbAvailDl` | 5.1.1.2.5、5.1.1.2.6 | 資料傳輸平均使用的 PRB 數、平均可用 PRB 數（上行對應 5.1.1.2.7、5.1.1.2.8） |
| Mean number of RRC Connections／Max number of RRC Connections | `RRC.ConnMean`／`RRC.ConnMax` | 5.1.1.4.1、5.1.1.4.2 | RRC connected 使用者數的平均與最大值，物件 NRCellCU |
| Mean number of Active UEs in the DL per cell | `DRB.MeanActiveUeDl`（加篩選條件） | 5.1.1.23.1 | 依 TS 38.314 彙總；5.1.1.23.5 說明 active UE 是「有資料等待傳送」的 UE |
| PDCCH CCE Usage per cell | `RRU.CceTot` | 5.1.1.2.19.1 | CCE 使用百分比 |
| Average DL UE throughput in gNB | `DRB.UEThpDl` | 5.1.1.3.1 | 單位 kbit/s |
| Wideband CQI distribution | `CARR.WBCQIDist.BinX.BinY.BinZ` | 5.1.1.11.1 | 用來確認無線品質有沒有變 |

課綱常說的「等待排程的使用者」，在標準量測裡最接近的是 active UE 數：有 DRB 資料等待上行或下行傳送的 UE（clause 5.1.1.23.5）。RRC 連線數只代表「連著」，不代表「有資料要送」。

**2. 為什麼 PRB 決定速率。** TS 38.214 V18.11.0 clause 5.1.3.2 計算傳輸區塊大小時，先算 N_RE＝N'_RE × n_PRB（n_PRB 是分給該 UE 的 PRB 總數），再算 N_info＝N_RE·R·Qm·υ（[TS 38.214](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.214/38214-ib0.zip)）。所以一個 UE 每個時槽能送的資料量，大致是「分到的 PRB 數」乘上「每個 PRB 的效率」（由 MCS 決定的碼率 R 與調變階數 Qm）。速率下降只有兩條路：PRB 分得少（容量），或效率變差（涵蓋、干擾）。PRB 在頻域怎麼分配，見 clause 5.1.2.2（下行）與 6.1.2.2（上行）。

**3. 判斷步驟。**

1. 看時間型態：速率下降是否只出現在忙時（5.4.3），離峰就恢復。
2. 看負載：`RRU.PrbDl` 偏高，同時 active UE 數與 RRC 連線數上升；再看分布型量測，避免平均值掩蓋短時間滿載。
3. 看品質有沒有變：CQI 與 MCS 分布和基線差不多 → 容量；CQI 分布往低端移 → 先查涵蓋或干擾（6.1.1、6.1.2）。
4. 看分母：`RRU.PrbAvailDl` 有沒有變少。可用 PRB 下降可能來自頻寬設定或其他組態變更，那是 6.1.6 的問題，不是需求暴增。
5. 看控制通道：`RRU.CceTot` 也高時，代表同時要排程的 UE 多，控制資源也吃緊。

#### 實際例子

甲電信（虛構）體育館周邊 NR 小區，3.5 GHz、100 MHz、SCS 30 kHz，數值虛構。100 MHz 在 30 kHz 下的最大傳輸頻寬是 273 個 RB（TS 38.101-1 V18.15.0 Table 5.3.2-1，[TS 38.101-1](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.101-1/38101-1-if0.zip)）。「CQI≥10 比例」是業者由 `CARR.WBCQIDist` 換算的報表欄位（虛構）：

```
日期：2026-11-22（演唱會），比較平日同時段基線
量測                   S501-1 基線   S501-1 20:00-21:00   S502-2 20:00-21:00
RRU.PrbAvailDl         273           273                  273
RRU.PrbUsedDl          120           257                  246
RRU.PrbDl (%)          44            94                   90
RRC.ConnMean           180           410                  95
RRC.ConnMax            236           523                  130
DRB.MeanActiveUeDl     9             36                   11
RRU.CceTot (%)         35            81                   40
DRB.UEThpDl (kbit/s)   62000         8600                 7400
CQI>=10 比例           63%           61%                  19%
```

逐步判斷：

1. 自洽檢查：257 ÷ 273 ≈ 94.1%、246 ÷ 273 ≈ 90.1%、120 ÷ 273 ≈ 44.0%，和 `RRU.PrbDl` 一致；可用 PRB 沒變，排除分母縮小。
2. **S501-1**：PRB 使用率從 44% 升到 94%，active UE 從 9 升到 36，RRC 平均連線數從 180 升到 410，CCE 使用率 81%；平均每個 active UE 分到的 PRB 從約 13.3 降到約 7.1，使用者速率掉到基線的約 1/7。CQI 分布幾乎不變（63% → 61%）。結論：容量與排程資源不足。
3. **S502-2**：PRB 一樣高達 90%，但 RRC 連線數與 active UE 數都不多，平均每個 active UE 用了約 22.4 個 PRB，CQI≥10 只有 19%。代表 UE 效率很低、每筆資料得用更多 PRB，**這不是容量問題**，要轉 6.1.1／6.1.2 查涵蓋或干擾。
4. **不能下的結論**：表中沒有上行量測，不能判斷上行是否也滿載；S501-1 有沒有鄰區可分擔負載，要另看鄰區的負載量測。

#### 容易搞混的地方

1. **PRB 使用率高不等於容量不足。** 涵蓋差或干擾大的 UE 用低 MCS，送同樣的資料要更多 PRB（TS 38.214 V18.11.0 clause 5.1.3.2），一樣會把 PRB 吃滿；要搭配 UE 數與 CQI／MCS 分布才能分辨（[TS 38.214](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.214/38214-ib0.zip)）。
2. **RRC 連線數不等於排程壓力。** RRC connected 的 UE 不一定有資料要送；active UE 數（有資料待傳）才接近「等待排程的使用者」。
3. **平均值會掩蓋尖峰。** 以小時為單位的平均可能只有六、七成，TS 28.552 說明分布型量測適合判斷小區是否在很短時間（例如 1 ms）內處於高負載（clause 5.1.1.2.3）。
4. **可用 PRB 變少看起來也像容量不足。** 頻寬或組態改變讓分母縮小時，使用率會升高，但根因在組態（6.1.6）。

#### 和其他小節的關係

忙時與基線的判讀見 5.4.1、5.4.3，流量與容量趨勢見 5.4.4，KPI 的讀法見 5.1.2。容量與涵蓋、干擾的區分見 6.1.1、6.1.2；上行受限造成的上行慢見 6.1.10。提出「容量不足」假設後，用 6.2.2 列反證（例如 CQI 是否變差）。調整負載分擔或擴容屬於 6.3.2 需要批准的處置，走第 9 章異動流程。量測定義見 [TS 28.552](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/28552-ic0.zip)。

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.2.1～5.1.1.2.8、5.1.1.2.19.1、5.1.1.3.1、5.1.1.4.1、5.1.1.4.2、5.1.1.11.1、5.1.1.23.1、5.1.1.23.5（[連結](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/28552-ic0.zip)）；TS 38.214 V18.11.0 clause 5.1.2.2、5.1.3.2、6.1.2.2（[連結](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.214/38214-ib0.zip)）；TS 38.101-1 V18.15.0 Table 5.3.2-1（[連結](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.101-1/38101-1-if0.zip)）

## 練習題

> 補充練習題（約 105 題）尚未交件，完成後放上。

### GSMA 練習題（88 題）

**1.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**3.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**4.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**8.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**12.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**16.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**20.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**24.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**37.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**50.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**59.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**68.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

**81.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.

User plane drive test data as follows：

**85.**

Analyze the 5G wireless network drive-test user plane data and engineering parameters.
Identify the reason for the throughput dropping below 600Mbps in certain road sections.
From the following 8 potential root causes, select the most likely one and enclose its number in \boxed{} in the final answer.

C1: The serving cell's downtilt angle is too large, causing weak coverage at the far end.
C2: The serving cell's coverage distance exceeds 1km, resulting in over-shooting.
C3: A neighboring cell provides higher throughput.
C4: Non-colocated co-frequency neighboring cells cause severe overlapping coverage.
C5: Frequent handovers degrade performance.
C6: Neighbor cell and serving cell have the same PCI mod 30, leading to interference.
C7: Test vehicle speed exceeds 40km/h, impacting user throughput.
C8: Average scheduled RBs are below 160, affecting throughput.

Given:
- The default electronic downtilt value is 255, representing a downtilt angle of 6 degrees. Other values represent the actual downtilt angle in degrees.

Beam Scenario and Vertical Beamwidth Relationships:
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to Default or SCENARIO_1 to SCENARIO_5, the vertical beamwidth is 6 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_6 to SCENARIO_11, the vertical beamwidth is 12 degrees.
- When the cell's Beam Scenario is set to SCENARIO_12 or above, the vertical beamwidth is 25 degrees.
